 "There is a reason I have agreed to meet with you, traveller, despite our disparity in stature. I have a request."= "旅行者，我能无视你我之间的地位差异屈尊与你见面，是有理由的。我有一个请求。"
 A busker practices a few extracts from Carmen, preparing for the next auditions at the Sidhe theatre.=一位街头艺人正在练唱《卡门》选段，为即将在希茜剧场举行的试演做准备。
 A cheer rises from the pub, where every hour is happy.=酒馆里传来一阵欢呼——那里每时每刻都是欢乐时光。
 A cloaked figure watches the world go by from the shadows, occasionally scribbling notes.=一位披着斗篷的身影藏身暗影中，静静观察世事，偶尔在纸上匆匆写下几笔。
 A pair of poets walk past, the blemmigans on their heads intertwined tendrils.=两位诗人从你身边走过，头顶的真菌虫触须彼此缠绕。
 A poet stands in the square, performing a depressing piece of their own creation.=一位诗人在广场上朗诵自己创作的忧郁诗作。
 A series of explosions in the distance tell everyone that theEnclave students are hard at work.=远处连串爆炸声告诉众人，飞地的学生正埋头苦干。
 A young poet scurries around, putting up posters for the new show at the Sidhe Theatre.=一位年轻的诗人满场跑，张贴希茜剧场新剧目的海报。
 An arrogant Cavy Knight in a rat-pulled carriage forces its way through the crowds.=一位趾高气扬的豚鼠骑士坐着由老鼠拉动的马车，硬生生挤过人群。
 Bombastic music from the Great Exhibition echoes through the streets.=来自大展会的喧嚣音乐在街道间回响。
 Children run up from the gardens below, their smiling faces smeared in vivid fruit juices.=孩子们从下方的果园跑上来，笑脸上沾满了鲜艳的果汁。
 Do not fear that I will come to harm. I am well-equipped to defend myself" – it fixes you with unblinking eyes – "and you, should the need arise."= 别担心我会受伤。我有充足的手段保护自己。"它用那对毫无眨动的眼睛直视着你，"必要时，也能保护你。"
 Happy splashing noises from below suggest that the water today is perfect for a bracing swim.=从下方传来的欢快水花声暗示着今天正是畅游的好日子。
 Over in Puck's Corner, a new orator steps up to try their luck with the crowd.=在帕克之角，一位新的演说者登台，试图赢得听众的青睐。
 People grind their teeth at the squeal of the Scientific Enclave's great telescope turning to face a new part of the sky.=天文望远镜缓缓转动，对准新星域时所发出的尖锐摩擦声，让不少人咬紧了牙关。
 The smell of fresh scones wafts over from the Salon.=沙龙那边飘来了新出炉司康的香气。
 Two actors practice their lines in the rainbow shade beneath the dome.=两位演员在穹顶下的彩虹阴影中练习台词。
 You have earned the respect of the Unassailable Founder for your excellent lecture. Perhaps now she will tell you more about herself.= 你的出色演讲赢得了无懈可击的创始人的尊重。也许现在她会告诉你更多关于她自己的事情。
 You recognise it. The locket used to belong to Captain Whitlock. Did she give it to the spirit?=你认得它。这个挂坠盒以前是惠特洛克车长的。是她把它交给鬼魂的吗？
 You spot a Servant in a mask of featureless jet, regarding the procession with an air of cool detachment. The rules of society seem to be suspended here.= 你看到一位戴着漆黑无貌面具的仆人正冷漠地注视着游行。这里似乎已没有社交规则的束缚。

 “I think we have it. A cup! A chalice – not necessarily hers, but proof she was worshipped here! Why would Sumerian worship spread here? Did the ancients have a path to the stars. So much we don’t know - but we are about to reveal at least one mystery under heaven.”=“我想我们找到了。一个杯子！一只圣杯——未必属于她本人，但至少证明她在此受人敬拜！为何苏美尔的信仰会传至此地？难道古人真有通往星辰的道路？我们尚有太多未知——但至少，有一桩天上的谜团，即将揭晓。”
"...This being cannot change." The Servitor's hollow monotone somehow manages to convey impatience. "Worlds change. Boundaries change. Winds and gods and laws change. It is in the nature of the Servitor to weather these changes and remain the same."="…仆从不能改变。"灵仆空洞的单调语调中竟隐含几分不耐烦。"世界会改变，疆界会改变，风、神明、律法皆会改变。而灵仆的本质，正是经受这些改变，而保持自我不变。"
"...for the mouth of the wicked and the mouth of the deceitful are opened against me: they have spoken against me with a lying tongue. They compassed me about also with words of hatred; and fought against me without a cause."=".……因为恶人的嘴，和诡诈人的口，已经张开攻击我。他们用撒谎的舌头对我说话。他们围绕我说怨恨的话，又无故地攻打我。"
"All's well at the moment, Captain."="目前一切正常，车长。"
"And thank you for the pleasant conversation, captain. The sky can be a lonely place. You enlivened it. I have included a small additional sum in your remuneration, as a token of my gratitude."="谢谢，和你谈话很愉快，车长。天空可是一个孤独的地方。你让它充满活力。为表示我的感激之情，我在你的报酬中额外增加了一笔小费。"
"Are you feeling more aware of the truth?" asks the Supervising Devil hopefully.="你是否感觉对真理更加敏感了？"魔鬼监督者满怀期许地问道。
"As he clothed himself with cursing like as with his garment, so let it come into his bowels like water, and like oil into his bones."="他拿咒骂当衣服穿上。这咒骂就如水进他里面，像油入他的骨头。"
"Captain, you'd best not be hanging around here. Wouldn't want you caught by the peelers. Don't worry about me. Someone's got to fight the good fight."="车长，您最好别在这附近逗留。我可不想让你被警察逮住。别担心我。总得有人去打这场正义之战。"
"Cleanliness," says the Supervising Deviless, "is next to... well. It appeals to the Judgments, anyhow!"="清洁，"女魔鬼监督者说，"几乎接近——嗯，不管怎样，它会讨司命们的欢心！"
"Death is inevitable," intones the Supervising Devil. "For your sort, anyhow."="死亡不可避免，"魔鬼监督者沉声说道，"对你们这种存在而言，尤其如此。"
"Do you feel any more interesting now?" asks the supervising Devil.="你觉得现在自己变得更有趣了吗？"魔鬼监督者问道。
"Do you mind?" the Penitent demands, looking your way. "This is hard enough without an audience." The supervising Devil is delighted by this outburst.="请问您能否回避一下？"忏悔者转头看向你，语气咄咄逼人，"这已经够难受了，不需要旁观者。"魔鬼监督者则因这番爆发显得异常欣喜。
"Everything in its proper place," says the Supervising Devil.="万物皆应归于其所，"魔鬼监督者说道。
"Heard rumours they're increasing their sweeps of the docks, Captain. Something to do with us, I'll bet!"="听说他们正在加紧巡查码头，车长。我敢打赌，这事儿跟咱们有关！"
"Hold not thy peace, O God of my praise..."="我所赞美的神阿，求你不要闭口不言……"
"How splendid," declares Mistress Fathom. "Something you need, in exchange for something that I may one day want." ="太好了，"英寻太太大声地说，"你所需之物，与我未来可能索求之物，交换成立。"
"I am gone like the shadow when it declineth: I am tossed up and down as the locust. My knees are weak through fasting; and my flesh faileth of fatness."="我如日影渐渐偏斜而去。我如蝗虫被抖出来。我因禁食，膝骨软弱。我身上的肉，也渐渐瘦了。"
"I believe we are starting to see some success," reads the Loquacious Civil Servant's latest note. "Whatever you are doing, I highly advocate its continuation and\u002For escalation, to be deleted as appropriate and with the greatest possible respect."="我认为我们开始看到一些成果了，"饶舌的公务员在最新的便条中写道，"不管您正在做什么，我都强烈建议继续进行并\u002F或加以扩大（请视情况删除），致以最高敬意。"
"I don't care if you think it's monstrous of me," grumbles the schoolteacher, trembling as she smokes her cigar. "But I'd rather shoot myself than have kids of my own." Unsupervised, her pack of prepubescent students rampage through the queue, barking inane questions.="我不在乎你眼里的我是不是残忍，"一位教师一边抽着雪茄一边咕哝道，声音颤抖，"但我宁愿一枪崩了自己，也不想生孩子。"在她的放任之下，一群年幼的学生在队伍中横冲直撞，嘴里喊着些莫名其妙的问题。
"I think we've all learned from this," remarks the Supervising Devil.="我想我们都从这中间学到了一些东西。"魔鬼监督者说道。
"I trust you see," remarks the Supervising Deviless, "why self-restraint is needed."="你现在明白了吧，"女魔鬼监督者说道，"为何自制是如此的重要。"
"I'm sorry," says the Deformer. "You broke a rule. Don't worry. I can get you out of here in no time, and in a much more pleasing shape."="对不起，"异形人说，"你违反了规则。别担心，我很快就能带你离开，而且会让你以更合意的形态离开。"
"I'm sorry," says the Reformer, dropping his head. "You broke a rule. I wish I could have warned you."="对不起，"改过者低下头说，"你违反了规则。我真希望我能早点警告你。"
"If I could just ask a few questions," says the Supervising Devil finally. "How are you feeling about the Great Chain of Existence? Positive, negative, or neutral?"="我能问几个问题吗？"监察魔鬼终于开口，"你对存在巨链的看法如何？积极、消极，还是中立？"
"If you were to see a dying bird," asks the Supervising Deviless, "would you rescue it?"="如果你看到一只垂死的鸟，"女魔鬼监督者问道，"你会去救它吗？"
"Is this making you at all more ambitious?" asks the supervising Devil curiously.="这样是否激起了你的雄心壮志？"魔鬼监督者饶有兴趣地问道。
"It lost its identity and its purpose," says the Conformer without emotion. "It has changed, but not enough, and it has diminished far more. Ignore it. It is useless now."="它失去了身份与目标，"顺应者面无表情地说，"它变了，但还不够，反而削弱了太多。别理它。它现在毫无用处。"
"It lost its identity without discovering a new one," says the Reformer sadly. "It has changed, but not enough, and it has diminished far more. Ignore it. There is no hope for it."="它失去了自身，却没能获得新的身份，"改过者悲伤地说，"它变了，但还不够，反而削弱了太多。别理它。它已经没有希望了。"
"It lost its shape and didn't acquire a new one," says the Deformer sadly. "It has changed, but not enough, and it has diminished far more. Ignore it. There is no hope for it."="它失去了形体，也没有获得新的样貌，"异形人悲伤地说，"它变了，但还不够，反而削弱了太多。别理它。它已经没有希望了。"
"It's a thing that lost itself," says the Performer with a grin. "It changed, but not enough, and it has diminished far more. Only a few things can save it now — a story, perhaps, or a name."="它是个迷失了自我的东西，"表演者咧嘴笑着说，"它确实改变了，但不够，而丧失了太多。现在只有极少数事物能拯救它——也许是一则故事，或者一个名字。"
"Just a warning, Captain. Lots of peelers down below. Watch yourself when you're out and about."="提醒您一句，车长。下面有很多警察。您在外面活动时要小心。"
"Let his children be fatherless, and his wife a widow. Let his children be continually vagabonds, and beg: let them seek their bread also out of their desolate places."="愿他的儿女为孤儿，他的妻子为寡妇。愿他的儿女漂流讨饭，从他们荒凉之处出来求食。"
"Let that be a lesson to you," says the Supervising Devil.="愿你从中吸取教训。"魔鬼监督者说道。
"Light, air, fire and self control are the enemies of pestilence," says a Supervising Deviless in a sing-song voice.="光、空气、火焰，还有自制，是瘟疫的天敌。"女魔鬼监督者用歌谣般的语调说道。
"Not to worry you, Captain, but I reckon we're making a bit too much noise. No big problem yet, but... just keeping you informed."="不想吓您，车长，但我觉得咱们动静有点太大了。现在还不算大问题，但……只是提醒您一声。"
"Nothing much to report, sir or ma'am. Ready for action when you are."="没什么特别要汇报的，先生或女士。您下令，我就采取行动。"
"One of our adherents here has written a remarkable poem about the collapse of a war-torn civilisation. Only afterwards, in the stillness, is there peace." The Orator seems genuinely touched. "Can you deliver it to New Winchester and ensure its publication?"="我们的一位追随者写了一首关于战乱文明崩溃的杰出诗作，唯有在废墟的寂静中，和平才得以降临。"演说家似乎被深受触动，"你能将它送去新温彻斯特，并确保它得以发表吗？"
"One of our local artists has painted a depiction of a forest of perfectly dead trees beneath an azure sun," says the Orator. "It's a very moving piece. I've arranged for it to sell at auction at Magdalene's – can you transport it there as soon as possible?"="我们的一位本地艺术家绘制了一幅描绘湛蓝太阳下一片死寂森林的画作，"演说家说，"这是一件令人动容的作品。我已安排它在抹大拉之家出售——你能尽快将它送去吗？"
"One of our more passionate scholars has written a series of explosive theses about the Philosophy," says the Orator. "Sometimes, one needs to be provocative in order to make a point. I was thinking that perhaps you could nail them to the door of the New Sequencer church on the Clockwork Sun."="我们一位更为激进的学者撰写了一系列关于哲理的激进论纲，"演说家说，"有时候，为了阐明观点，就必须挑衅。我在想，也许你可以把这些论纲钉在发条太阳上的新秩序者的教堂门上？"
"Perhaps a memory from childhood will help you warm up," the Supervising Deviless suggests.="也许从童年的记忆中找一找线索会有帮助。"女魔鬼监督者建议道。

"Still inclined to falsehoods," writes the Supervising Devil.="依旧倾向于谎言，"魔鬼监督者写道。

"Those who read the Song of the Sky shall not leave unscathed!"="一位本地天空车长在空井边举行黑暗仪式的诡异故事！"一名商贩高喊，将你的小册子像圣典般在空中挥舞。"魔法与疯狂！" 另一人起劲地喊道， "凡读《天野之歌》者，必受灵魂震颤！"
"The mutations?" Has he seen you glance? Did he spot you notice the woman with the gills, the man with the tendrils instead of fingers? "They are a blessing. A product of prayer and ritual and immersion in the mists Only those accepted by They Who Must Grieve are so touched."="突变？"他看到你瞥见的东西了吗？他有没有注意到那个长着鳃的女人，以及那个手指变作卷须的男人？"它们是一份祝福。是祈祷、仪式和沉浸在迷雾中的产物。只有那些被执哀者接纳的人才会如此。"
"The scandalous tale of a rogue MP!" declares one salesman, waving copies of your pamphlet in the air like a holy text. "Infamy and intrigue!" shouts another with relish. "The captain who conquered Parliament!"="流氓议员的丑闻故事！"一名商贩高喊，将你的小册子如圣典般举过头顶挥舞。 "恶名和阴谋！" 另一人起劲地喊道， "征服议会的车长！"
"There is such a thing as too much, wouldn't you say?" asks the Supervising Devil hopefully.="凡事都有个度，不是吗？"魔鬼监督者带着一丝期许问道。
"These patients are the most difficult," says the Supervising Devil aside to you. "Except maybe those in the Stunted Grove. It's so hard to refine a soul that's gotten into this state."="这些病人最难对付，"魔鬼监督者低声对你说，"也许得除开矮小荆棘丛那边的病人。他们的灵魂堕落得太深，精炼起来太难了。"
"This is what happens when you ask too many questions," remarks the Supervising Devil.="这就是你提太多问题的后果，"魔鬼监督者冷冷地说。
"Tragically, things aren't going to plan. We can't afford another expedition, and so we languish here, making what we can out of what we've been able to find thus far. It will not do."="可悲的是，事情并没有按计划进行。我们没有能力再进行一次探险，所以我们在这里苦苦挣扎，用我们迄今为止所能找到的东西来做我们能做的事。这不可能成功。"
"Try to allow yourself to feel some emotion," prompts the Supervising Deviless.="试着让自己感受一些情绪，"女魔鬼监督者引导道。
"We await at Wolvesey Station."="我们在伍夫塞站等候。"
"We don't have the resources to mount any more expeditions, so I'm left with what meagre evidence I've been able to gather. It convinces me, for sure, but I can't imagine Summerset will look kindly upon it."="我们已无力组织新的探险，只能依赖我所收集到的那点微薄证据。它对我而言已足够有说服力，但我无法想象夏暮会正眼瞧它。"
"We go to the Throne of the Hour-King."="我们去了时间之王的王座。"
"We go to the messenger-corpse; that squirming ruin of tumbled spires."="我们去了信使尸体，那摇摇欲坠的尖塔废墟。"
"We go to the regent's obelisk, which you have made into a gaudy amusement."="我们去了摄政王的方尖碑，那个被你们变成俗丽娱乐场的地方。"
"We go where roses bloom in the dark."="我们去了玫瑰盛开的黑暗之地。"
"We journey to the place of apples, where an old regent's ruins lie."="我们前往苹果之地，那里有一个古老摄政王的遗迹。"
"We journey to your house of invention, near the Ormswold."="我们去往你们的发明之屋，在涡野附近。"
"We return to the smoggy, clanking, singing, stamping, thronging, frantic stew-pot of humanity; the blister of glass and steel in the heart of the Garden."="我们回到烟雾缭绕、叮当作响、歌唱、脚步、拥挤、疯狂的人类大乱炖——花园之心的玻璃与钢铁泡沫。"
"When he shall be judged, let him be condemned: and let his prayer become sin. Let his days be few; and let another take his office."="他受审判的时候，愿他出来担当罪名。愿他的祈祷，反成为罪。愿他的年日短少。愿别人得他的职分。"
"Would you say you are feeling more or less conscious of your mortality than when you began this programme?" asks the Supervising Devil.="你认为，与这次改造开始前相比，你对自己的死亡感更强还是更弱？"魔鬼监督者询问道。
"You broke a rule," says the Conformer. "You should have been more careful."="你违反了规则，"顺应者说，"你应该更小心些。"
"You must go to Lustrum," says the Gardener-Archivist. "Once there, read the Unflower. You'll know what to do."="你需要前往伍载镇，"保管故事的园丁说，"在那里再次阅读未开之花。你会知道该做什么的。"
"You must go to the Most Serene Mausoleum, in Albion," says the Gardener-Archivist. "Once there, read the Unflower. You'll know what to do."="你需要前往阿尔比恩的圣宁墓，"保管故事的园丁说，"等你到了那里，再次阅读未开之花。你会知道该做什么的。"
"You must take this to the Eagle's Empyrean in Eleutheria," says the Gardener-Archivist. "Once there, read the Unflower. You'll know what to do."="你需要把它带到厄琉忒里亚的雄鹰之苍天上国，"保管故事的园丁说，"等你到了那里，再次阅读未开之花。你会知道该做什么的。"
"Your debt is paid," the Inhabitor's voice-jar intones. "If you wish, you may return to your chariot, and the way will open."="汝债已偿，"门槛住民的声音罐低声说道，"若汝有意，可归战车。道途将为汝而启。"
'A Forensic Assessment of the Sun's Remains'=《对太阳遗迹的医学评估》
'A breakthrough! I worked late into the night, burning every candle to keep myself warm  The budget can spare it, and d__n the Bursar. There are gaps between the stars, where stars once were. This must be the origination of the Discordance. Our enemies maneuver, out there in the cold and dark, and conspire against itself. But the Azure, the enemy at the Gate, has not turned its gaze away. I feel its light like breath on my neck, I feel its Law. It hates us. I must warn London to only send astronomers whose usage of hours is moderate in future.'='一个突破！我彻夜苦干，燃尽每一根蜡烛以求取暖。预算还能撑得住，去他的财务主管。星星之间出现了空隙，那里曾有星辰。这一定便是不谐的源头。我们的敌人正在那寒冷黑暗之地演习，阴谋诡计，甚至自相对抗。但那蔚蓝，那门前之敌，尚未移开目光。我能感到它的光，就像呼吸贴近后颈；我能感到它的法则。它憎恨我们。我必须警告伦敦，今后只派那些对时间使用尚属节制的天文学家前来。'
'A small measure of progress was achieved. I fixed the telescope firmly on the Horizon, as I had indicated previous. Much must be left to speculation, but I believe there are commonalities with my observances at Port Avon. A different intent, for sure, but one must wonder, for how long have our microscopic activities been under the lens of minds immeasurably superior to our own?'='取得了一点小小的进展。如先前所言，我已将望远镜牢牢对准了地平线。虽仍需推测，但我相信它与我在埃文村的观测存在某种相似之处。当然，意图或许截然不同，但我们不得不想一想：人类的这些微不足道的活动，到底被远胜于我们的生物监视了多久？'
'A thought that has stricken me. Felled me almost. If the stars in their conjunctions are alliances, if the chain is across as well as above and below, what we have is in fact, a lattice. A matrix, if you will. A web.'='我突然产生了一个几乎让我瘫倒的念头。如果星辰之间的连结是一种联盟，如果巨链贯穿上下，那么我们所拥有的其实是一个格子。或者说，一个矩阵。一个网络。'
'Another discovery! This involved some tricky Weft-work, and a very steady hand. But, examining the constellations as I observed them from what might have been millenia ago, and comparing them with the placement of the rock on Polmear & Plenty's, was instructive. I have consulted geologically minded friends. But the theory that regions of the Wilderness are bounded locuses must now be called into question. Incidentally, I am having regrets regarding the absence of my own soul.'='又有一项发现！这需要些高难度的纬线操作，以及一只极其稳定的手。但我将如今看到的星座与或许是千年前的图像进行比对，再将它们与波尔梅尔与普伦蒂的岩石位置对照，结果极具启发性。我已咨询了几位有地质学背景的朋友。但‘外野的各区域是固定界域’这一理论，现在恐怕要被重新审视了。顺便说一句，我对自己灵魂的缺失感到有些后悔。'
'I have let my crew die, while I lived.'='我让我的列车员死去，而我却活着。
'I have let my crew die, while I lived:' and then their names begin to scrawl themselves over one another like a grisly wordsearch.='我让我的列车员死去，而我却活着：'然后他们的名字开始像一个可怕的字谜一样互相涂抹。
'I have lost count of how many of my crew have died. I cannot remember their faces.' Then the names of the crew that have died under your command begin to sear themselves into your skin, in a dense scribble.='我已经记不清楚我的列车员死了多少人了。我不记得他们的脸。然后在你的指挥下死去的列车员的名字开始在你的皮肤上烙印，密密麻麻的涂鸦。
'I was commended at graduation for my tenacity, my nerve, and my lack of imagination. I find myself returning to those virtues more and more lately. I have ordered the telescope directed entirely at the azure. We feel the full force of its glare. I see shades, spirits, phantasms. Sometimes, I think I am being toyed with. Just the other night, I awoke to a beam of blue starlight, right over the scope. I crept out there, and stood, alone, on the edge of night. I felt judgement. All that I am could be boiled to atoms and scattered to the farthest edges of the horizon, and still it would pursue me until not even the wind dares howl my name. And once I am less than the smallest molecule of dust, it would forget me utterly, its attention fixed on some grander design. This is the nature of our enemy.'='毕业时我因坚韧、勇气与缺乏想象力而获赞。这些美德，近来我愈发依赖。我已下令将望远镜完全对准蔚蓝。我们正承受它目光的全然注视。我看见影子、幽魂、幻灵。有时，我觉得自己成了玩物。就在前几晚，我被一道蓝色星光唤醒，正投射在镜筒上。我悄悄走出，独自站在夜之边缘。我感受到审判。我的存在可被分解为最细微的粒子，四散至天边尽头，而它仍会追逐我，直至连风都不敢再呼唤我的名字。待我连尘埃都不如时，它便会彻底遗忘我，将注意力投向更宏大的计划。这，便是我们的敌人之本质。'
'Incandescent fire on the horizon. A warning? A welcome? Sometimes I think they speak in sigils to confound us. Dolton has proved useless, as ever. Perhaps I shall make a pyre of his books and use the ashes to draw the Correspondence in my notes. All this is to say, the heavens remain an enigma, searing, splendid, and sibylline.'='地平线上燃起炽白的火焰。是警告？还是欢迎？有时我觉得它们用符印说话只是为了迷惑我们。多尔顿一如既往地毫无用处。或许我该把他的书烧成灰，然后用灰烬在我的笔记中画信符。总而言之，苍穹仍是谜团，炽烈、瑰丽而晦涩。'
'Nothing to report. Another day twiddling my thumbs. Hurry up and get me a permit from the Ministry if you want to actually learn something, you idle sot. Yours, the Professor.'='无事可报。又是无所事事的一天。你这懒鬼，要是真想学点东西，就赶快去从公德部那里给我弄份许可来。此致，教授。'
'Outrage! Calumny! Perfidy! These were the phrases that stood out most in the Bursar's Annual Report. I had some choice words for him too, as you can well imagine. It is ridiculous that we know so little about our own territories. Their former lords may be vanquished and cast down, but their effects are as tangible as the distant starlight that dapples the boughs of Traitor's Wood. They think me eccentric, but I find their ignorance mad. Why watch the borders when we have no idea what creeps about inside our walls?'='丑闻！中伤！背叛！这些词在书记员的年度报告中最为显眼。我当然也有些妙语送给他，正如你所想象的那样。我们对自己领地的了解如此之少，简直荒谬。那些昔日的领主也许已被击败、被驱逐，但他们留下的影响依旧真实可感，如同叛徒林枝头上斑驳的星光。他们觉得我是个怪人，但我倒觉得他们的无知才是真正的疯狂。为何要监视边境，而我们却连自己墙内潜伏着什么都毫不知情？'
'Progress! Slow and inexorable as the spinning of a spider's web, but progress all the same. A stray observation based on the patches of luminosity in the sky, and the correspondent darkness between others. The constellations are politic.'='有进展了！就像蜘蛛结网一样缓慢而不可阻挡，但进展毕竟是进展。一次偶然的观测记录了天空中明亮斑块的分布，以及其间对应的黑暗。星座之中存有政治。'

'The Condemned Classics: an International Perspective'=《被谴责的经典：国际视角》
'The work continues sporadically. It would help if London would put a bit more support behind me, rather than casting its nets where-ever it thinks it might get a bite. I was appointed as their expert. But no matter, as soon as I get my hands on the telescope again, I'll turn my attentions back to our enemy in Azure.'='研究进展断断续续。如果伦敦能多给予些支持，而不是遍地撒网碰运气，情况也许会好些。我可是他们任命的专家。不过，算了，只要能再次掌管望远镜，我便会重新将注意力转向我们在蔚蓝之地的敌人。'
'There are old prayers. I was thinking about this the other night, when I was at the telescope, alone. The other lazy blighters were long abed. And I was gazing up, where the stars lie enmeshed in their webs, like flies spun in silk, but who eats them? What spiders might crawl on what terrible legs and creep about the heavens, dangling on Correspondence like thread, inching closer and closer to their incandescent meat. But, of course, there are no such spiders. Unless they were introduced. And how do spiders breed, why, they lay eggs! A second thought: to whom, in this event, would the spiders pray?'='我知道一些古老的祷文。前些夜里我独自在望远镜前时想到的——其他那群懒骨头早早就去睡了。我一个人仰望星空，那些星辰被困在网中，如同蚊蝇缠绕于丝中。但又是谁吃掉它们？是哪些蜘蛛，长着可怖的腿，在天穹之上蠕行，借着信符的蛛丝悬挂前行，缓缓靠近它们那炽热的肉食？当然了，这世上是没有这种蜘蛛的……除非，它们是被引入的。蜘蛛如何繁殖？没错，它们产卵！第二个念头是：若真如此，这些蜘蛛又会向谁祷告？'

A macaron store! Delighted, you bustle towards the cramped stall, shouldering past an old man in a top hat. But before you can accost a shopkeeper, you realise they're actually just cardboard cutouts pasted on cheese so old the mould is iridescent. How disappointing. =一家马卡龙店！你满心欢喜地挤过人群，快步走向那间狭窄的摊位，甚至不小心撞到了一个戴礼帽的老人。然而，你还未来得及向店主搭话，便发现这些马卡龙只是些粗糙的纸板工艺品，贴在一块陈年老酪上，酪上的霉菌闪烁着五彩斑斓的光泽。太令人失望了。

A maze of low ceilings and opaque black water. You've been convicted of a major infraction, and you will need to change a great deal before you are released.=一个低矮天花板和黑色浊水构成的迷宫。你因严重违规而被判刑，必须做出巨大改变后方可获释。
A moment of hesitation, and then a dam bursts in your mind. Words flood the page and carry you forward in an irresistible current, until you are flung into an ending as though dashed upon rocks. The result is rushed and imperfect. Grimly, you go back. You knock the story down and rebuild it, brick by painstaking brick, working with the nihilist fury of a master blacksmith forging his own guillotine. Finally, just as you are ready to throw your entire manuscript into the fire, you realise that you no longer need to – there's nothing left to fix. The Sixth Canto is complete.=片刻的犹豫后，你脑海中的闸门猛然打开。文字如洪流般席卷纸张，推动你不断向前，直至你被抛入一个突如其来的结局，如同被摔碎在礁石上。结果仓促而不完美。你咬牙回头，从头打碎重建，一字一句，如同一个狂热的工匠在锻造自己的断头台。终于，就在你几乎准备将整个手稿投入火中时，你意识到——已经无可再改。第六章完成了。

After the Titanians have all filtered in, the rules are set: every half hour, each participant will be given a glass of wine. If they do not finish it by the time the next round is served, they are disqualified. The last one remaining wins. What happens next, however, is uncertain. The identity of the winner is a mystery, as is the identity of the one who stole the Empress' bust from its glass case. As a point in fact, the whole week afterward is blurry. But you're certain it was a fine time, after the headaches end.=在狄坦妮娅人全部过滤完毕后，规则就定下来了：每隔半小时，每个参与者将得到一杯酒。如果他们在下一轮酒上桌时没有喝完，就会被取消资格。最后剩下的人获胜。然而，接下来会发生什么，是不确定的。赢家的身份是一个谜，就像从玻璃柜中偷出皇后半身像的人的身份一样。事实上，之后的整个一周都是模糊的。但你可以肯定那是一段美好的时光，在令人头疼的结束之后。
After the Titanians have all filtered in, you are allowed a great honour: to stand on the bar, lay out the rules, and - by your sole discretion - decide the victors. Your arbitration bolsters some and destroys others. But truly, most of them should feel no shame: the quality of the lyrics they fish from their mugs is far superior to what one hears in London. Except for the songs of the Red-feathered Warbler. Light in hand, you wander the empty cabins and carriages. The floor of the boiler room is scarred with scorch marks. The captain's cabin is littered with pieces of furniture. There are bullets in the walls. There are no coffins aboard, but the hold is locked. A great groaning emanates from within.=你提着灯在空荡荡的车舱和走廊中游走。锅炉房的地板上有烧焦的痕迹。车长室里杂乱无章，满地家具碎片。墙上嵌着子弹。车上没有棺材，但货舱上锁了。从里面传来一声低沉的呻吟。
Light music wafts through the air of Titania's natural petal-pool. On the banks, painters try to capture the natural beauty of the place, while skinny-dipping poets and playwrights glide with various degrees of grace through the water. A veritable Eden like this is no place to be encumbered by the weight of either fabrics or modesty.=狄坦妮娅肥沃的花瓣肉质被证明极适合种植水果。在水晶穹顶投下的光束中，巨型草莓闪耀光泽。拳头大小的蓝莓垂挂在鼓胀的灌木上。树枝间，苹果、橙子与梨如宝石般悬挂，只待成熟之际采摘。

For the most part it is a place to tell stories rather than air grievances, with poets and comedians testing out new material free booing or ballistic tomatoes.=帕克之角是一座小型公园，在这里，言论自由不受限制。但口若悬河者须谨记：当你从字面意义上的“肥皂箱”上下台后，你所说的话依旧会被铭记。总体而言，这里更像是说书人的乐园，而非抱怨者的角落——诗人、喜剧演员可在此测试新作，不必担心嘘声或迎面飞来的番茄。
Puck's Corner is ready to hear any opinions.=帕克之角已准备好聆听各方论点。

It's a story of blood and battle and betrayal, of a dark sky lit by explosions, and of how you played a decisive role for the Tacketies in the midst of the carnage.=第四章讲述撕裂天穹的温彻斯特战争。这是一个关于鲜血、战斗与背叛的故事，描绘了被爆炸点亮的黑暗天空，以及你如何在腥风血雨中为钉鞋党力挽狂澜。

The Fourth Canto is the tale of how you discovered the long-lost Parzifal, cementing your place in the pantheon of history's greatest explorers.=第四章讲述了你如何发现失踪已久的帕西法尔，从而巩固你在历史上最伟大探险家殿堂中的地位。
The Fourth Canto reaches mythic proportions – here, the Song becomes the story of a captain who spat in the faces of gods, and lived through terrible consequences to tell the tale. It's a triumph of the most blasphemous kind.=第四章触及神话——在这一章，天野之歌讲述了一位车长侮辱众神，并承受可怕后果的故事。车长的幸存是那些最为渎神之人的胜利。

The Great Exhibition takes up an entire dome, offering visitors a look at all the wonders of both London and the High Wilderness. Steam engines rumble and hoot. The Heiress won't leave without her sister. Find the Counsellor's agent at Perdurance and seek advice.=女继承人要求和她姐姐一起离开。找到恐怖顾问的密探并寻求建议。
The Hermit accepts your offering and gestures for you to join them; to soothe the dying animal's pain in its last moments. They sift their fingers through its insides, plucking out organs and placing them on the ground in a careful but indecipherable pattern. When it is complete, the Hermit rests a compassionate hand on the beast's head and slits its throat in a smooth, practised manner. What they hoped to learn from this goes unspoken, but as you stand to leave, you see the pattern. An eye.=隐士接受你的提议，打手势示意让你加入，来减轻濒死动物在最后时刻的痛苦。他用手指在动物的体内筛选，取出器官，以一种小心但晦涩难明的图案放在地上。完成后，隐士同情的将手放在野兽的头上，并以平稳、老练的方式切开它的喉咙。他希望从中学习的东西并未说明，但当你站起准备离开时，你看到了图案——一只眼睛。
The Hermit cups their hands beneath a gentle trickle of foul smelling water. They drink, then their words flow freely. They speak of the great charnel houses of Europe. How the Eagle will fight the Bear. How the Clockwork God will fall. A whisper of your own future too, never intended for your ears. A warning of what you may become in the dark.=隐士把手放在一股散发着恶臭的细流下。他只有饮下流水，才能连贯自由的说话。他谈及欧洲最大的埋骨地，鹰如何与熊搏斗，发条之神因何陨落。一个关于你自己未来的耳语，从未想过会出现在你的耳边；一个关于你在黑暗中可能会变成什么的警告。
The Hermit cuts a purple flower from their shoulder, licking the blood from the stem. They wrap it tight and place it in an old chipped cup full of gathered rainwater, stirring with the obsidian shard until it runs dark. They offer it. There is no refusing. When you wake up, only blessed fragments of nightmare remain. Burning at their core is an eye; the same one you now see repeated again and again in the Hermit's garden of flesh.=隐士从他的肩膀上割下一朵紫色的花，舔着茎上的血。他把它包得紧紧的，放在一个有缺口的旧杯子里，杯子里装满了收集的雨水，用黑曜石细条搅拌，直到天黑。他提供这个，无法拒绝。当你醒来时，唯有噩梦仍存。在他的心脏中燃烧着的是一只眼睛，那只你在隐士的血肉之园上反复看到的。
The Hermit drags a sharp rock through their flesh, carving spiral patterns on already bloodied skin. Their milky-grey eyes stare at something beyond the cave wall. Their whispers could as easily be prophecy or madness.=隐士拉拽着一块锋利的石片穿过他的身体，在已经鲜血淋漓的皮肤上雕刻出螺旋式的图案。他乳灰色的眼睛似乎盯着洞穴外的什么东西。他的低语似乎能轻易做出预言，抑或是招致疯狂。
The Hermit finishes carving their latest spiral and takes a drink from a gentle trickle of water running down from the roof of the cave. Their milky-grey eyes stare past you; their mouth occasionally forms a coherent word. Sweat builds on their brow. Their words might be truth, falsehood, or any flavour of madness in between.=隐士完成了他手上的螺旋图案的雕刻，并啜饮从洞穴顶部缓缓流下的细流。他乳白色的灰色眼睛凝视着你，偶尔说些什么。汗水积聚在他们的额头上。他的话可能是真的，也可能是假的，抑或是介于两者之间的呢喃疯语。
The Hermit lingers at the edge of sight, their rags the remnants of a zee-captain's uniform. Two of their eyes are closed tight. The third, carved with scars into their arm, seems to stare through you.=隐士徘徊在视界的边缘，他身上的破布是海泽船长制服的残余部分。他双眼紧闭，而刻满疤痕的手臂上的第三只眼睛好似紧紧注视着你。
The Hermit sits motionless, meditating under a steady drip of water that feeds their garden of flesh. Their unblinking eyes pay you no attention as you take the candle deeper into the cave. Roses bloom in the dark, curling up and dying in the presence of the light.=隐士一动不动地坐着，在源源不断的水滴下冥想，这滴水滋养了他的血肉之园。当你持着蜡烛深入洞穴时，他那不眨的双眼没有注意到你。玫瑰在黑暗中绽放，却光明面前蜷缩着枯萎。
The Hermit whispers to the darkness. They pay you no mind as you look at their few remaining possessions. A trading log from a place far away, a faded photo of a love last seen in Old London. A diary that begins with scholarly descriptions of Eleutherian wonders and ends with nothing but scribbles of the same eye carved into their flesh. The last coherent entry is dated November 1886. =隐士对着黑暗低语，并不在乎你看到他仅剩的资产——一份遥远地区的交易日志，一张在旧伦敦最后一次与爱人相见所拍的褪色的照片。一份以厄琉忒里亚景色的学术性描述为起点，以胡乱涂鸦其身体上镌刻的眼睛为结尾。最后一个连贯的条目日期是1886年11月。
The Hermit works their haruspicy on the dying beast, occasionally pausing to stroke its fur and close its eyes to the pain. Their cave is filled with bones and rotting meat and a smell that makes your eyes water. Amongst them is the wreckage of what appears to be a zubmarine. A second glance at the bones reveals that not all of them are from animals.=隐士在濒死的野兽上实施他的脏卜术, 偶尔停下来抚摸它的皮毛，使它在疼痛中闭上眼睛。他的洞穴里满是骨头和腐烂的肉，还有一种让你流眼泪的气味。其洞穴大致是一艘潜艇的残骸，再看一眼这些骨头，就会发现并非所有骨头都来自动物。
The Hermit's puckered skin is a mass of fungal blooms and the purple-leafed flowers of twilight. They tend to them with a sliver of obsidian, delicately carving weeds from their flesh.=隐士那皱缩的皮肤是由一团团菌落般的，在暮色映衬下叶色艳紫的花朵相接而成。他乐于以黑曜石的细条在血肉上精心雕刻，去除芜杂。
The Hermit's words are rapid in pace and low on coherence. They speak while painting a picture on the wall; another eye, like the one in their tattoo. Now your vision has adjusted, just a little, to the darkness, you can see the same symbol emblazoned a thousand times across the walls, sometimes open, often closed. Just for a moment, you understand. Just for a moment, you see.=隐士的话语节奏快却不连贯，他一边在墙上画画一边说话，而另一只眼睛，就像他们纹身上的那只。你的视力稍微适应了黑暗，可以在墙上看到同样的符号，其中有些是开放的，有些是闭合的。仅一会儿，你看到了，仅一会儿，你理解了。

No detours."=包罗万象的出版商叼着雪茄，将你的手稿举到灯光下端详起来。他手腕上的手表珠光闪烁（他什么时候开始戴这种东西了？）。"今晚你应该庆祝一番，"他一边倒了一大杯威士忌，一边说道。"然后直接回家，开始写下一篇。其他的哪里也别去。"

The Reverberant Activist=声如洪钟的行动者
The Rhapsode begins in the early morning, and does not finish until the week is over. Every day he speaks, in a tale expertly crafted; every word in its place, every character saying the right thing - everything is just and perfect in his vision.=狂想家从清晨开始，直到一周结束都没有结束。每天他都在说，在一个专业制作的故事中；每一个字都在它的位置上，每一个角色都在说正确的事情--在他的视野中，一切都公正而完美。
The Ringmaster's calliope blasts its noisy tune, but even it is almost inaudible over the roar of the guests out front. The performers are taking turns to put on free shows, much to the delight of their visitors. Back here, they clink glasses in celebration and devise new wonders for the future guests who will come.=团长的蒸汽风琴轰然作响，却不敌前排观众的兴奋叫嚷。演员轮番上阵，献上免费表演，引来观众连连叫好。演出结束后，他们又在后台住宿区干杯庆贺，也构思着为下一轮观众准备的新奇表演。

The Royal Horologist raises an eyebrow. "A white city indeed? What strange treasures the old power kept locked away. No doubt the old rogue copied us."=皇家时计员扬起一边眉毛。"真有白城这地方？旧势力还真藏了些稀罕宝物。毫无疑问，那老家伙一定是在模仿我们。"
The Royal Horologist sighs unhappily. "The power that previously claimed dominion here was, by all accounts, regrettably showy. Carrying on like a pantomime potentate. Not that I've seen one." =皇家时计员不快地叹了口气。"先前在此处逞威的那股力量，据说实在过于浮夸。活像个滑稽剧里的暴发户。当然，我本人倒是没亲眼见过。"
The Royal Horologist smiles reverentially. "Ah, the Promised Days. A happier time, before there was all this mucking about."=皇家时计员的微笑带着敬意。"啊，应许之日。那是一段更加快乐的时光——在这一切变得混乱之前。"

The Second Canto is a roaring yarn of a blood-spattered train, of teeth gouging into iron, of smoke and death and gunpowder. It's a story that leaves the reader exhausted and elated.=第二章是一部有关血迹斑斑的火车、凿进钢铁里的兽齿、烟雾、死亡与火药的紧张故事。它让读者精疲力竭，却又兴奋不已。
The Second Canto is a story of your time as a Member of Parliament — the intrigue, the scandal, the dizzying bureaucracy! It's farce and satire and tragedy, rolled into one.=第二章讲述了你担任国会议员期间的故事 — 阴谋、丑闻、令人目眩的官僚机构！它集闹剧、讽刺剧和悲剧于一体。
The Second Canto is the blood-stirring story of your overthrow of the Brabazon regime. A song of blood and banners and shattered chains, and tyrants trampled beneath the feet of the people.=第二章讲述了你推翻布拉巴宗政权的血腥故事。这是一首血腥之歌，充满了旗帜、破碎的锁链，以及被人民践踏的暴君。
The Second Canto is the story of a hostage rescued from a tyrant Empress. It's a hero-narrative, yes, but also a ruthless exposé of the corruption at Albion's core.=第二章讲述了从暴君皇后手中救出一个人质的故事。这是一个英雄故事，但同时也无情地揭露了阿尔比恩内心的腐败。
The Second Canto is the story of a prison beyond understanding. A labyrinth with arbitrary rules and enigmatic gatekeepers that alters you beyond recognition. It's the story of an escape, yes, but also of what was left behind.=第二章讲述了一个令人费解的监狱。那是一个迷宫，规则随意独断，看守神秘莫测，让你面目全非。这不仅是有关一场逃亡的故事，还是一个有关所留下的事物的故事。
The Second Canto is the story of how you became the Knight of Clocks, crusading across the skies in the name of the Horological Office, imposing order on chaos.=第二章讲述了你如何成为钟表骑士，以时计部的名义征战天空，使混乱恢复秩序的故事。
The Second Canto is the story of how you resurrected the legendary wreck of the Boatman, and brought the dead to their final destination.=第二章讲述了你如何复活了传说中的船夫号，并引渡活死人至终焉之地。
The Second Canto is the story of how you saved the Clockwork Sun from the Empire's neglect, and rescued the sky from darkness. The Sun plays the role of both villain and hero, squatting at the centre of the story in all its decaying majesty.=第二章讲述了你如何从帝国的忽视中拯救发条太阳，以及如何从黑暗中拯救天空的故事。太阳既扮演着恶棍的角色，又扮演着英雄的角色，以其腐朽的威严蹲伏在整篇故事的中心。
The Second Canto is the story of the Winchester War which tore the skies in half. It's a story of blood and battle and betrayal, of a dark sky lit by explosions, and of how you played a decisive role for the Stovepipes in the midst of the carnage.=第二章讲述撕裂天穹的温彻斯特战争。这是一个关于鲜血、战斗与背叛的故事，描绘了被爆炸点亮的黑暗天空，以及你如何在腥风血雨中为高帽党力挽狂澜。
The Second Canto is the story of the Winchester War which tore the skies in half. It's a story of blood and battle and betrayal, of a dark sky lit by explosions, and of how you played a decisive role for the Tacketies in the midst of the carnage.=第二章讲述撕裂天穹的温彻斯特战争。这是一个关于鲜血、战斗与背叛的故事，描绘了被爆炸点亮的黑暗天空，以及你如何在腥风血雨中为钉鞋党力挽狂澜。
The Second Canto is the tale of how you became the Saint of Wells – a story to chill the bones of the reader, to make them lift their eyes to the stars above in wondrous dread.=第二章讲述了你如何成为井之圣徒的故事——这个故事会让读者毛骨悚然，让他们带着惊奇的恐惧仰望头顶的星空。
The Second Canto is the tale of how you discovered the long-lost Parzifal, cementing your place in the pantheon of history's greatest explorers.=第二章讲述了你如何发现失踪已久的帕西法尔，从而巩固你在历史上最伟大探险家殿堂中的地位。
The Second Canto reaches mythic proportions – here, the Song becomes the story of a captain who spat in the faces of gods, and lived through terrible consequences to tell the tale. It's a triumph of the most blasphemous kind.=第二章触及神话——在这一章，天野之歌讲述了一位车长侮辱众神，并承受可怕后果的故事。车长的幸存是那些最为渎神之人的胜利。

The Servitor has changed its nature.=灵仆已经改变了它的性质。
The Servitor has either removed its mask or grown flesh over it. Its face is anxious, and the colour of a pearl. "I don't know what I am any more," says the Audacious Servitor. "Except free."=灵仆或是摘下了面具，或是长出了覆盖其上的肉体。它的脸上写满焦虑，肤色白如珍珠。"我已经不知道自己是什么了，"大胆的灵仆说道，"只知道我自由了。"
The Servitor is grateful for your sacrifice on its behalf.=灵仆对你代其承受的牺牲心怀感激。
The Servitor's porcelain mask is still impassive, but its long fingers flex and twitch and spasm. "See? I have not changed at all," it says grumpily. Its voice is no longer the thudding of stone. It sounds like a mildly peeved accountant.=灵仆的瓷面依旧冷漠，但那双长指却在不安地抽动、颤抖、蜷缩。"看见了么？我一点没变。"它闷闷不乐地说。它的声音不再是石块坠地之声，而像是一位略显恼火的会计师。

The Sun's rays wash over Albion from corner to corner, like a pale tide. Its light waxes and wanes, sometimes burnished gold, sometimes grey-gold as a parliamentarian's beard. The camp is abuzz with activity. Errant young medievalists flood the grove, busying themselves erecting tents and arguing the finer points of the ablative case. The Dismal Paleographer looks almost happy, lecturing his new flock on the principles of Chivalry and Feudalism. The Vituperative Classicist glowers by the fire. The Feckless Theologian looks tremendously put out.=营地里一片喧嚣。稚气未脱的中世纪研究者挤满林间空地，忙着搭帐篷，并为夺格变化的细微差别争得面红耳赤。阴郁的古物学家看起来几乎称得上愉快，正向他的新门徒讲授骑士精神与封建主义的基本原则。挑剔的古典学者在火边怒目而视，软弱的神学家则显得极其不高兴。
The camp is alive with plainsong. Hymns sound through the incense-choked trees. Young theologians have converged on Traitor's Wood, debating the respective merits of Boethius and Aquinas. The Feckless Theologian is in the thick of the activity, providing wine and commentaries on the most daring heresies. The Vituperative Classicist and the Dismal Theologian sit at opposite ends of the camp, scowling at their gathered piles of evidence.=营地中响起平调圣歌。圣歌声穿过烟雾缭绕的树林。年轻神学家聚集于叛徒之林，争论波伊修斯与阿奎那的思想孰优孰劣。软弱的神学家活跃其间，为最具胆识的异端思想提供酒水与注解。挑剔的古典学者与阴郁的古物学家则分坐营地两端，阴沉地盯着自己那一堆堆的证据。
The camp is deserted. A cold wind blows the ashes off the campfire. A few torn pages from a monograph, remorselessly scribbled over, are torn away. A nightingale calls from somewhere in the dark wood. Rain is beginning to fall, washing away the detritus left behind.=营地空无一人。一阵冷风将篝火上的灰烬吹散。几页潦草涂写的专著残页被风卷起。林中夜莺啼鸣。雨开始落下，冲刷着留下的残骸。
The captain describes a field of debris they flew through to reach the signal box. Each rock was bursting with life. Tiny flowers the colour of milk jostled in the currents of the engine. The locomotive was overwhelmed with the scent of honey, and home.=车长记述了他们在抵达信号箱途中穿越的一片碎石带。那里的每一块岩石都充满了生命。乳白色的小花在机车的气流中摇曳。整辆列车弥漫着蜂蜜与家的香气。
The captain describes the dapple of distant starlight falling over their engine; the long hours spent in the watchful dark, suddenly shattered by a dazzle of cold radiance. When will they know such splendour again?=车长描述着远方星光洒落在机车上的斑驳光影。他们在警觉的黑暗中度过漫长时光，直至被一道冷峻的光辉所打破。他们何时才能再见如此光芒？
The captain details various damages done to their engine, and the causes. Hull: aggrieved cantankeri. Backplate: bad launch off Port Prosper. Frontplate: bad landing on Port Prosper. Hull again – Jones. A change in personnel gives the captain optimism for the next leg of the journey.=车长详细记录了机车所遭受的各类损伤及其原因。车体：受到愤怒的顽固虱撞击；后板：从兴荣港起飞时撞歪；前板：在兴荣港降落时撞歪；再一次，车体 —— 是琼斯的责任。人员调动使得车长对接下来的旅程抱有希望。
The captain warns of a condition afflicting her crew: it started during a period of downtime. One took up gazing at the stars, and encouraged the others into consideration of the heavens. This has since become a fixation, and the entire crew only grudgingly leaves the windows to perform their labours.=车长警告称，她的列车员受到了某种影响：最初是在一次休整期间开始的。有一人开始凝视星空，并鼓动他人也开始面对苍穹沉思。自那时起，这种行为便变得根深蒂固，全体列车员如今都只在窗前凝视的空隙勉强前去履行职务。
The captain writes feverishly of a light that danced at some distance before them. After some hours following it, it abruptly grew, getting brighter as it did. Then nothing, only the sense of something lost without a chance to understand.=车长狂热地写下那团在远处跳动的光。跟随它数小时后，它突然变大，愈发明亮。然后便什么都没有了，只剩下某种失落之感，来不及理解便已错过。
The captain writes feverishly of a light that danced at some distance before them. After some hours following it, it abruptly grew, getting brighter as it did. Then nothing. Just— Gone.=车长狂热地写下那团在远处跳动的光。跟随它数小时后，它突然变大，愈发明亮。然后——什么都没有了。就这样……消失了。
The captain writes of a homestead their locomotive passed when journeying from Port Avon. The buildings were in the style of old London. The nostalgia brought the crew together, after a near disastrous encounter with a Chorister bee.=车长写道，他们的列车从埃文村出发，途中曾经过一处宅地。那些房屋是旧伦敦的风格。这份怀旧之情使列车员们重新团结在一起 —— 此前他们才与遭遇一只唱诗蜂，几乎酿成灾难。

The first lecture you stumble upon has attracted a vast crowd, and you must fight to even get to see the speaker. He has a dejected posture, his voice is quiet but insistent. "Who made the Halved forgo its obligations?" he asks. "Who was the storyteller whose words extinguished a star?"=你偶然走近的第一个讲座吸引了一大群听众，你甚至得挤进去才能看到演讲者。他姿态黯然，声音虽轻，却坚定不移。"是谁让那位“半分者”放弃了自己的义务？"他问，"谁是那位用言语熄灭了群星的说书人？"
The footman disappears into the gloom, leaving you alone facing a desk shaped like a baptismal font. A Cheery Registrar sits in the middle, waiting to greet you.=男仆消失在黑暗中，只留下你一个人面对着一张形似洗礼池的桌子。一位热情的登记员坐在中间，等待迎接你。
The frames shop, as it turns out, is not where you purchase corrective glasses. Instead, it hawks poor metaphors and second-rate similes, phrases with which to inadequately frame a situation. Not wanting to be impolite, you buy an analogy for an upset stomach and ignore the way they harvest fresh stock from a gaggle of weeping men.=所谓的“画框店”，其实并不售卖矫正眼镜，兜售的是蹩脚的隐喻和拙劣的比喻，勉强能用来“勾勒”某种境况。为了不显得失礼，你买下了一个形容胃部不适的比喻，并对店员从一群哭泣的男人身上采集新商品的行为视而不见。
The frames shop, as it turns out, is not where you purchase corrective glasses. Instead, it hawks poor metaphors and second-rate similes, phrases with which to inadequately frame a situation. Not wanting to be impolite, you buy an analogy for an upset stomach. =所谓的“画框店”，其实并不售卖矫正眼镜，兜售的是蹩脚的隐喻和拙劣的比喻，勉强能用来“勾勒”某种境况。为了不显得失礼，你买下了一个形容胃部不适的比喻。 
The gland your crew found was dried up and almost worthless. But the Curator does not seem without hope. Speak to it aboard your engine.=列车员发现的腺体已经干枯无用。但是负责人似乎还没有绝望，上机车和它谈谈
The grand chamber of the Morning Room is overseen by a Dowager Duchess fairly dripping with emeralds. You're amazed she can lift her wrists.=晨间厅这座华丽殿堂由一位戴满祖母绿的老年女公爵主持。你简直无法想象她如何抬得起双腕。
The graveyard is at the end of this quiet street. Not many people travel here; the electric lights are fewer, barely keeping away the dark.=墓地坐落于寂静街道尽头。鲜有人迹至此；电灯稀疏寥落，勉强抵御着黑暗侵袭。
The grinding and guttering of the looms Below Stairs adds an additional layer of horror to an already terrifying procession.=楼下织机的轧响与嗤咯声，为这本就恐怖的行进增添了一层新的惊惧。
The ground gives way to a bog – grey and fetid as a newly slain corpse. It burps lazily. The marsh reeds susurrate, growing in volume as you approach. There is English amidst the polyphony: names – yours and those of your crew.=地面塌陷成一片沼泽——灰色而腐臭，如同一具刚死的尸体。它懒洋洋地冒着泡。沼泽的芦苇发出沙沙声，随着你的靠近，声浪越来越大。那混杂的低语中竟夹杂着英语：是人名——你的名字，还有你列车员们的名字。
The handful of shades on your crew cluster together at your end of the table, pulling their masks fractionally forwards to admit their forks. You are careful not to catch a glimpse of what lies beneath. Your living crew sit at the other end of the table, talking quietly among themselves.=你列车员中为数不多的几位遗魂聚在餐桌一端，稍稍将面具向前拉开，以便将叉子送入口中。你小心不去窥视面具下隐藏的东西。活着的列车员们坐在桌子的另一端，低声交谈。
The homes are equally lovely. They could have been plucked from some far-away idyll on Earth, rather than here in the High Wilderness.=这些房屋同样可爱。它们仿佛是从地球上某个如牧歌般的远方土地摘取而来，而非来自这天野。
The hour hands on the clock in your cabin spin round and round again. Something chimes.=你舱室内的时针不停旋转，一圈又一圈。钟声响起。
The hour-harker looks embarrassed about bringing you here. Not enough to return your Sovereigns, of course.=时间探子带你来这里时显得有些尴尬——当然，这点尴尬并不足以让他退还你的金币。
The hovering instant between opening your mouth and saying something regrettable.=张口与说出令人遗憾话语间的徘徊瞬间。
The insistent cry of a nuthatch echoes through the wood=一只䴓鸟执着的啼鸣在树林中回荡
The insistent cry of a nuthatch echoes through the wood. =一只䴓鸟执着的啼鸣在树林中回荡。
The insistent shriek of a whistle. You find the whistle, abandoned, on the galley floor.=一声尖锐刺耳的哨声响起。你在厨房地板上发现了那只被人遗弃的哨子。
The journey is disturbed. As you traverse the relay-passage, some celestial beast thunders amidst the roots of the sky. Its lamentable bellows shake the heavens.=旅途受到扰动。当你穿越中继通道时，某只天界巨兽在天空的根系中雷鸣般穿行。它哀恸的咆哮震动苍穹。
The keening of the hours as they are spun backwards makes your legs weak and your heart pound. You feel unaccountably wearied.=时间被倒纺的哀鸣让你双腿发软、心跳加速。你感到莫名的疲惫。
The land here is so damp, anything built on it likely sank into the marsh many years ago.=这片土地潮湿至此，凡是建在这里的东西，多半早在多年前便沉入了沼泽。
The lanes bustle with visitors. The widow Campbell's guest house is turning people away. The Nowhere inn glows with bright lamps, and thumps with the sound of music-hall songs.=巷子里的游客熙熙攘攘。寡妇坎贝尔的客房已经住满。无处旅馆里灯火通明，内部音乐厅的歌声节奏顿挫。
The leader of your former cell is waiting on the bed in the room, his dark hair bound up in a bandana. The years have left him more scarred, but he still carries his hookah pipe and his little black book. "Suppose I could finally tell you a few names." He gives you a crooked smile. "Or maybe you're here for something a little more friendly like?"=从前的牢房里，你的领导人正在房间的床上等待，他的黑发用头巾束起。岁月给他留下了更多的伤痕，但他仍然带着他的烟斗和他的小黑书。"我终于可以告诉你几个名字了。"他给了你一个狡黠的微笑。"或者，也许你来这里是为了一些更友好的事情？"
The light fails. The roses sicken, wither, and are gone.=光芒消逝了。玫瑰们生病，枯萎，凋零了。
The light is a flushed yellow, autumnally ripe.=光线泛着红润的黄色，宛如秋日的成熟果实。
The light is thin, winter-bright.=光线微弱，色如冬日。
The light is warm, a reminder of summer.=光线温暖，令人想起盛夏时节。
The light's the colour of a good dessert wine, uncorked in autumn.=光线的色泽如一瓶上好的秋酿甜酒。
The lights of the port slowly fade. Beyond lies a forested grey swamp, misted over and filled with leafless black trees that claw at the air.=港口的灯光渐渐远去。前方是一片灰色的森林沼泽，迷雾笼罩，枯黑的树木仿佛利爪般撕扯着空气。
The longer you spend in Worlebury-juxta-Mare, the easier it is to spot the flaws in the facade. There! Another detail, not quite right.=你在滨海沃伯瑞呆的时间越长，就越容易发现其表面的缺陷。在那里！另一个不大对劲的细节。
The maggots under your skin are quiet, for now.=你皮下的蛆虫暂时安静了下来。
The magician could be at Port Prosper.=魔术师可能在兴荣港。

The marsh seems to go on forever. Increasingly it feels as if it actually might.=沼泽仿佛永无尽头。这种感觉正变得越来越真实。
The marsh-gas shimmers in front of your eyes. It stings. They bleed tears.=沼气在你眼前闪烁。它刺痛着你。你的眼眶中流出血泪。
The masked dead now comprise so many of your number that some of the living crew have begun wearing masks and swearing vows of silence to fit in. It makes for a solemn, gloomy meal, the only sound the clink of cutlery on bowls and on porcelain masks.=亡者的数量如今已多到使部分活着的列车员也开始戴上面具，并发誓沉默，以融入他们之中。晚餐气氛肃穆而沉郁，唯一的声音来自刀叉碰撞碗碟与瓷面具的清响。
The masked dead sit at one end of the table, the living at the other. The dead lift their masks forward to spoon food to their cold lips. The living crew are careful not to look. It's considered bad luck to glimpse the faces of the dead. You suppose new superstitions bloom quickly under these conditions.=亡者戴着面具，坐在餐桌一端；生者则坐在另一端。亡者将面具稍稍掀起，用勺将食物送至冰冷的唇边。活着的列车员刻意避免目光接触。看到亡者的脸被认为是不祥之兆。在这样的环境下，新迷信总是迅速滋生。
The memory of another world begins to fade. Albion. London. The Reach. The stuff of dream. A childish fantasy.=另一个世界的记忆正逐渐淡去。阿尔比恩。伦敦。外野。梦之物语。孩童的幻想。
The mess has been converted into a nursery. Your Chief Engineer seems content here, which is a small mercy.=食堂已成了一个托儿所。你的轮机长似乎对当前的处境感到满意，这算是一种小小的慰藉。
The mid-morning calm is shattered by furious yelling in the distance. "The Psalmists are causing trouble again!" shouts a passing urchin joyously.=清晨的宁静被远处暴怒的叫喊打破。"诗篇教派又在惹事啦！"一个路过的顽童高兴地喊道。
The mist thickens. Shadows move in the dark.=雾气加重。黑暗中，有影子在移动。
The mists tickle. You paddle listlessly through their depths, breathing in the candy-sweet. This is really quite pleasant. =雾气拂过你的身子。你无精打采地划过雾的深处，呼吸着那糖果般的甜香。这感觉……相当惬意。
The mixture is about to set.=混合物即将凝固。
The mixture is becoming harder to stir.=混合物逐渐变得难以搅拌。
The mixture is hardening quickly.=混合物正在快速硬化。
The mixture is liquid and runny.=混合物呈液态且流动性好。
The mixture will not take any more additions.=混合物无法再添加任何物质。
The mountain giveth...=山的贡物…
The mountain sings no more.=大山不再歌唱。
The mud bubbles. The air here is poison.=泥潭沸腾，空气中满是毒气。
The mud clings to you. It burns slightly. Itches.=泥巴紧贴着你的皮肤，轻微灼痛，令人发痒。
The mud is hard here, covered with soft snow. A few young trees flourish in the inhospitable conditions, but not many. Only one other mining group seems to have staked a claim here. The hour-harker warns that it would be most impolite to ask about their success.=此地泥土坚硬，被一层柔软的积雪覆盖。一些幼树在恶劣环境中勉强生长，但数量不多。似乎只有另一组矿工在此认领了一块地。时间探子提醒道，询问他们是否成功是非常不礼貌的行为。
The mud is soft here, and the ground fertile. Though outside the boundaries of your prospective claim, giant trees spear through the snow and rise high over your head. It is close enough to town for off-duty miners to chop them down for firewood, much to the cheer of the chilly miners in Lustrum town.=这里泥土松软，土地肥沃。虽然这些巨树并不在你预定认领的范围内，但它们仍矗立在积雪中，高耸入云。此地离镇子不远，下班的矿工会来砍伐这些树作为柴火，这让伍载镇中受冻的矿工们颇为高兴。
The mud is soft here, under thick snow. There is no vegetation to be seen. Many claims have been staked here and their owners continue to work them.=雪下泥地松软，四周寸草不生。已有许多认领地设于此地，其主人们仍在继续挖掘。
The mud under your feet bubbles furiously. A fetid smell of dead flesh rises up.=你脚下的泥地剧烈地冒着泡。一股腐肉的恶臭扑鼻而来。
The names of all your old crew, and what became of them.=你以前所有船员的名字，以及他们后来的情况。

The only way forward is to forge through a bitterly cold river. Tiny creatures bite at your flesh the whole way.=你不得不涉过一条刺骨冰冷的河流，无数微小的生物一路啃咬你的皮肤。
The opinion columnist, who has notorious Tackety sympathies, makes acid-tongued references to 'the lapdog captains of London,' of which you presume you are one.=那位同情钉鞋党的意见专栏作家对'伦敦的哈巴狗队长们'发表了尖酸刻薄的言论，你觉得你自己会是其中之一。
The other lecture to catch your attention is being boomed across the Forum by a tall, grotesquely muscular man with a beard that explodes from beneath his mask. His voice is both extremely loud and mesmerizing in its sing-song cadence.=另一个吸引你注意的讲座正回荡在整个广场上，由一位高大、肌肉夸张的男子主讲。他面具下蓬勃生长的胡须仿佛要炸裂开来。他的声音既极其洪亮，又带着催眠般的吟唱节奏。
The other lecture to entice you is that given by a diminutive old woman, speaking in a dry whisper to only three Aspirants. But you catch the phrase "coils of shattering ice, a thousand feet high."=另一个吸引你注意的讲座是由一位身材矮小的老妇人主持的，她用干涩的耳语对仅有的三名有志者低声细语地讲着什么。但你听见了这样一句话："碎裂冰蛇，千尺高。"
The other lecture you come across is being delivered by a lithe, confident figure of indeterminate gender. They are expounding on their topic at such length that you have trouble deciphering what they're talking about. Finally, you identify the topic: a certain Mr Barleycorn.=你遇到的另一个讲座由一位身形灵巧、自信满满、性别难辨的人主讲。此人长篇大论地讲述着某个主题，让你一时间难以理解其意。最终，你才辨认出主题：麦寸先生。
The other lecture you notice is being delivered by a compact woman of unmistakably military bearing, with one leg that appears to have been replaced below the knee by a fire-poker. She is talking about something called 'the Binary.' A team of Recalcitrants are trying, but failing, to shout her down.=另一个被你注意到的讲座是由一位体格紧凑、具有明显军人气质的女子主持的。她的一条腿似乎在膝盖以下被一根火钩取而代之。她正在讲述某种被称为‘双生者’的存在。一群顽抗者正试图打断她，但显然未能成功。
The other lecture you stumble upon has attracted a vast crowd, and you must fight to even get see the speaker. He has a dejected posture, his voice is quiet but insistent. "Who made the Halved forgo its obligations?" he asks. "Who was the storyteller whose words extinguished a star?"=你偶然走近的另一个讲座吸引了一大群听众，你甚至得挤进去才能看到演讲者。他姿态黯然，声音虽轻，却坚定不移。"是谁让那位“半分者”放弃了自己的义务？"他问，"谁是那位用言语熄灭了群星的说书人？"
The other masked figures leave you a wide berth as you make your way down. When you turn to them, they flinch away, as though expecting you to launch into invective. =当你往下走时，其他戴着面具的人都离你远远的。当你面对他们时，他们会退缩，好像觉得你马上就要开始谩骂一样。
The pale gold light of spring shines through the windows.=春日淡金色的阳光透过窗户洒落进来。
The passage is less pleasant than you had hoped. A crewman swears he saw something vast stirring in the  sky's deeps, and raves about a "many-eyed burrower" to any who will listen.=旅途并不如你所希望的那样愉快。一名列车员发誓他在天空的深处看到某种庞然大物在蠢蠢欲动，并对愿意倾听的人喋喋不休地讲述那"多目的掘隧者"。
The path gives way to an expanse of quickmud. Only a few tufts of grass mark safe passage.=小路被前方的一大片流泥地中断。只有几块草丛标志着勉强可行的路径。

The tale has grown in the telling, and grown, and grown. In its vastness, it has become impossible to control. You find yourself searching through previous Cantos to ensure consistency of character and detail – stumbling unexpectedly upon narrative dead ends – writing yourself into terrible tangles that cannot be salvaged. With the grim determination of Hercules facing the hydra, you cut. And cut. Until the story lies bleeding, but better, at your feet. The Fifth Canto is complete.=这个故事在讲述中不断扩展，扩展，再扩展。它的广阔已经超出了你的掌控。你发现自己不得不翻阅先前的篇章，以确保人物和细节的连贯性——却意外地撞上了叙事的死胡同——陷入无法挽回的逻辑困境。你以赫拉克勒斯挑战九头蛇的坚定决心开始裁剪。砍去冗余，削减赘述，直到故事血淋淋地躺在你的脚下，却也变得更加完美。第五章完成了。

Themes that had once lurked in the recesses of your mind now rise unbidden to the surface, shaking the last vestiges of your subconscious from their flanks. You finally get the sense that you know what you're doing, and more importantly, where you're going. You sit to write, fingers flying, that shining spectre of an ending blazing like a beacon in your head... until finally, you reach it. The Fourth Canto is complete.=曾经潜伏在你意识深处的主题，如今不请自来，驱散了最后的潜意识余烬。你终于有了方向，知道自己在做什么，更重要的是，知道自己要去往何方。你坐下来，双手在键盘上飞舞，那个闪耀的终点像灯塔般在脑海中燃烧……直到你终于抵达。第四章完成了。

Words spit like lightning from your fingertips. The typewriter shudders and clanks in protest under the strain. You write in a kind of fury, your mind's eye raging with images of gloating stars and endless burning emptiness – and then you sit back, exhausted, as you realise you've run out of words. The Second Canto is complete.=文字如闪电般从你的指尖迸发。打字机在压力下咔嗒作响。你写作时仿佛陷入了一种狂热，脑海中充满了傲慢的群星与无尽燃烧的虚空——然后，你瘫倒在椅子上，精疲力竭，意识到自己已经写尽所有言辞。第二章完成了。
Work is slow on the new Salon, located just off Valentine Square. The Stone-Faced Sculptor waves away any questions or criticisms, determined only that whatever it turns out to be, it will be the finest example of it in the Reach.=新沙龙的施工进度缓慢，位置紧邻瓦伦丁广场。石面雕塑家对任何质询或批评都不屑一顾，唯一的坚持是——无论最终成品为何，它都将是外野中此类建筑的最佳典范。
Work is underway on the Academy for the Bloody Arts. It is not what they want; but it might be what they need.=血腥艺术学院的建设正在进行中。这或许不是他们想要的，但也许是他们所需要的。
Work is underway on the Apollo Fortress. May it offer the Titanians some protection.=阿波罗要塞的建设正在进行中。愿它能为狄坦妮娅人带来些许庇护。
Work is underway on the Astral Amphitheatre. Epic poetry will no longer be confined to pages alone; they shall be spoken again.=星空露天剧场的建设正在进行中。史诗将不再只被书写，它们将再次被高声吟诵。
Work is underway on the Empyreal Ballroom. The Titanians shall soon be dancing under the stars.=九天舞厅的建设正在进行中。不久之后，狄坦妮娅人将在星光之下起舞。
Work is underway on the Imperial Cathedral. The hymns they shall sing will ignite future choirs.=帝国大教堂的建设正在进行中。他们将吟唱的圣歌，必将点燃未来的赞颂之声。
Work is underway on the Library of Yore. The historians' art will find no finer home than Titania.=昔日图书馆的建设正在进行中。历史学家的技艺将在狄坦妮娅找到最合适的归宿。
Work is underway on the Light Opera House. Nowhere else in the Wilderness will you find comedy so incisive or penetrating.=轻歌剧院的建设正在进行中。在这片天野中，再无其他地方能呈现如此犀利深刻的喜剧。
Work is underway on the Lustful Palace. Love poems will blossom in its courtyards.=情欲宫殿的建设正在进行中。爱之诗将在其庭院中绽放。
Work is underway on the Observatory. The astronomical arts shall be practised here.=天文台的建设正在进行中。天体的艺术将在此重绽光辉。
Work is underway on the Queen's Head. The Titanians will have a home for their songs at last.=女王之首酒馆的建设正在进行中。狄坦妮娅人终于将有一个属于他们歌声的栖所。
Work is underway on the Saturnine Theatre. Titania shall soon have a home fit for Tragedy.=农神剧场的建设正在进行中。狄坦妮娅将迎来一个悲剧之家的落成。
Workers burst through into the Bit Between from the workworld. Overseers run in both directions – some fleeing, some throwing themselves into the melee.=工人们从工作世界冲进了夹缝之地。监管员顿时四散——有些逃命，有些则投入了混战。

You stare at the half-empty page in panic. For a moment, you fear you've lost your terrible momentum. You write until your fingers are blistered, until the ending catches you by surprise. You recline, sweat-drenched. The Third Canto is complete.=你惊恐地望着那半空的页面。片刻间，你害怕自己失去了那股狂热的冲劲。但随后，故事再度将你吞没，你化身为泥塑的傀儡、传讯的媒介、一个急于倾诉最后信息的幽灵。你不停地写，直到手指起泡，直到故事的终点猝然降临。你瘫倒在椅子上，浑身湿透。第三章完成了。

is chained to a chair with a practitioner of the psychological arts, who keeps asking questions about hidden desires and buried traumas.=被锁在椅子上，面对一位心理咨询师，他不断提问，个个关于内心深处的欲望与深埋心底的创伤。

is doing vigorous exercises in a sweaty room.=需要在一间闷热的屋子里做激烈运动。
is drinking the sort of poison that makes your lips go numb if properly dosed, and kills you in minutes if not.=正在饮鸩，适量的毒药会令嘴唇麻木，但稍有不慎便会致命。
is eating a preparation of giggling mandrake; it is hilarious when bitten.=正在吃会咯咯笑的曼德拉草，这东西被咬时确实令人发笑。

“Ghastly, isn’t it? I’ve never been able to abide classicists. They’re entirely dogmatic about their Latin, and have never read anything besides Homer and Virgil. And she’s holding court, of course. Just like back when - well. But surely you can see it’s all for nothing? There’s absolutely no way Inanna is here. After all, the whole ruddy point of the legend is she gets up again!”=“真是糟透了，不是吗？我从来就受不了那些古典学者。他们在拉丁文上固执得要命，除了荷马与维吉尔什么都不读。而她还在那里大讲特讲，简直就像当年——呃。不过你总该能看出这根本毫无意义吧？伊南娜怎么可能在这里？她的整个神话核心就是‘她会再度苏醒’啊！”
“I think I’m closer to having conclusive proof. I’d go to the tomb myself, immediately, but I need Summerset’s backing if my findings are to receive any academic legitimacy. As ever in academia, we are beholden to the purse-strings.”=“我想我已经接近确凿的证据了。我恨不得立刻亲自前往那座墓地，但若想让我的研究获得学术认可，仍需夏暮的支持。学术界一如既往地受制于金主。”
“If another snot-nosed professor emeritus in the making explains to me the origin of St John’s cult in Milan, I shall scream. I shall be so cross if he’s right. Although I must say, it's been nice to hear him singing the Song of Roland again. He has a very nice voice, and that was one of his favourites, once.”=“要是再来一个鼻子不好的教授来向我解释圣约翰崇拜在米兰的起源，我就要崩溃了。如果他说对了，我倒可真要生气。不过老实说，他又唱的《罗兰之歌》倒是挺好听。他嗓音不错，而且那曾是他的最爱之一。”
“It is good to have another face about. I am heartily sick to death of that pair, and not for the first time. They are convinced of such utter rot. Any fool could tell you that this forest is the resting place of Charlemagne. Every legend and romance I’ve read convinces me of it.”=“有新面孔真好。我已经彻底受够那两个了，而且这也不是第一次。他们坚信着一些彻头彻尾的胡言乱语。任何傻瓜都能看出这片森林就是查理曼的长眠之所。每一则传说与骑士故事都让我深信不疑。”
“It's good to see you! Your work in the woods has borne fruit. Summerset has sent me students - and funds - though, of course, not enough to mount another expedition. But that they’re willing to invest at all suggests they’re intrigued by what I’m - we’re - finding here. We shall find her, you and I, and her songs will sound in the heavens again.”=“见到你真高兴！你在森林里的努力结出了果实。夏暮大学派来了学生——还有经费——当然，不够再组织一次探险。但他们愿意投资，说明他们对我——对我们——在此地的发现颇感兴趣。你我定能找到她，让她的歌声再度响彻天穹。”
“I’m glad someone’s decided to talk to me, at least. I’m waiting to see how long it’ll take her acolytes to realise how pig-headed she is. If I have to hear another lecture on the similarities between the greek heroes and the saints, however, I shall scream and scream until Saint John gets out of his grave by himself.”=“至少还有人愿意跟我说话。我倒要看看她的信徒要多久才会意识到她有多固执。可如果我再听到一场讲座，把希腊英雄与圣徒们相提并论，我就要尖叫到圣约翰自己从坟里爬出来为止。”
“Must he carry on so ridiculously? Well, of course he must. God forbid restraint ever enter his stupid head. They’re all prim and proper now,  of course, these awful milk and wafer students – but I know a bacchanal in the making when I see one.”=“他非得这么夸张不可吗？当然，他非得如此。节制这种事从未出现在他那愚蠢的脑袋里。这些讨厌的吃牛奶与圣饼的学生们现在一副正襟危坐的模样，可我一看就知道，这注定要变成一次酒神祭。”
“My evidence, however, is sadly lacking, even if the theory is sound. The queen of heaven is surely situated among the stars. But why is she buried? Well, mythological time is not our time. And here so many things thought impossible reveal themselves to be entirely, magnificently possible. Perhaps this is the Underworld of the ancients.”=“不过，尽管理论合理，我的证据仍然十分匮乏。天后女神无疑栖居于星辰之间，但为何她会被埋葬？唔，神话中的时间并非我们所理解的时间。在此地，许多被认为不可能之事，都能以令人叹服的方式成为现实。也许这里正是古人所说的冥界。”
“Summerset’s been so excited by our findings they’ve sent me students! And a little money  – for their upkeep – and for an a proper study of our findings. Not enough for another expedition, of course. Too much material evidence frightens them. The Dean was an archaeologist, I think. He has quite a horror for it now. “=“夏暮对我们的发现激动不已，他们派来了学生！还带来些许资金——用以他们的食宿——还有对我们发现的适当研究。当然，也不足以再发起一场探险。他们对实证材料实在太过惧怕。校长曾是考古学家，估计就是因此才对考古有了阴影。”
“Thank Christ. Someone new. We’ve been trying to reach that tomb on the hill for positively forever. It’s on the far side of the wood, and we’re rather strapped for cash. Summerset’s refusing to send us a penny more for another expedition. They did not appreciate my last monograph. Apparently I brought infamy to the college. I was merely reflecting on our student days.”=“谢天谢地。终于来个新人。我们试图到达那座山丘上的坟冢已经太久了。它在林子的另一边，而我们几乎没钱可用了。夏暮拒绝再拨一分钱，说是我的上一篇专著给学院带来耻辱。但我只是回顾了我们的学生时代罢了。”
“Thanks to you, we have evidence of cultic activity. Which, I’m sure would delight my colleague. But the iconography is distinctively Johannine. If we just had a little more - Summerset might be willing to take this seriously. Perhaps even help fund us through to the grave itself!”=“多亏了你，我们有了邪教活动的证据。我确信，这会使我的同事们感到高兴的。但是，这些图画具有鲜明的约翰福音书风格。如果我们再多掌握一点--萨默塞特可能会愿意认真对待这个问题。也许甚至可以资助我们通过坟墓本身！”
“Things aren’t going as well as I’d hoped. According to the Legenda aurea, Saint John is sleeping at Ephesus until the end of the world. I suppose he means to see if he got it right in Revelation. As you may have noticed, we're not in Ephesus. But the apostles were scattered to the ends of the world. If anyone were to find their way to the stars, it would be him."=“事情并没有像我希望的那样发展。据《黄金传说》所说，圣若望将在以弗所沉睡至世界的终结。我想他期望看到自己在启示录中是否说对了。正如你可能注意到的，我们并非在以弗所。但使徒们分散到世界的尽头。如果有人能找到通往恒星的路，那人一定是他。"
“This is intolerable. If they’re not butchering poor Aquinas, they’re drinking themselves senseless. And I cannot imagine the nerve of that - that creature - to lecture anyone on sin and virtue! Leave them alone with enough wine, and they’ll soon forget all about Saint John and the barrow. That’s the whole problem with theology - it doesn’t have to prove anything!”=“我受不了了。他们不是在肢解可怜的阿奎那，就是在把自己灌醉。我真无法理解那家伙怎么还有脸向别人讲授罪行与德性！只要给他们足够的酒，他们很快就会把圣约翰和那座坟冢抛诸脑后。这就是神学的最大问题——它根本不用证明什么！”
“We are so close, I can practically smell him! Will he thank us, I wonder? Accept us as his disciples and let us sit at his feet and hear tales of the death of saints? A cup such as the Catholics use to signify the miracle of communion. I must write to the College. Surely now, they’ll support us.”=“我们已经非常接近了，我几乎可以闻到他的味道！他是否会感谢我们，我想知道？接受我们为他的弟子，让我们坐在他的脚下，听他讲述圣人之死的传说？一个像天主教徒用来象征圣餐奇迹的杯子。我必须给学院写信。现在，他们肯定会支持我们。”
“We’ve been here positively months and have nothing to show for it. We’re all but out of funds. I’ve done my bit, making sure the other two can never be taken seriously again  - but we’ve no money for supplies for ventures into the wood. We believe the barrow is the resting place of a legendary monarch. We just can’t agree on which.”=“我们在这里已经好几个月了，毫无所获。经费也快见底了。我已经尽力让那两个永远无法被学界当回事——但我们连再进森林一次的补给都没有。我们都相信那坟冢埋着一位传奇君主，只是各执一词，不知到底是谁。”
“You know I hold you responsible for this sepulchral bacchanalia.” She sniffs. “God, medievalists. Never seen so many corpses still walking about, and I’ve been to the Tomb-Colonies thrice. I simply cannot bear to see that fourth rate practitioner of a third rate discipline triumph.”=“你知道我全怪你让这场死人狂欢节继续下去，”她哼了一声，“中世纪学者，天哪。我去过古墓殖民地三次，都没见过这么多走来走去的尸体。我实在受不了那个三流学科的四流从业者得逞。”
“You see the dividends our work has produced! I have students of my own! Stipends! Grants! Though - between you and me - the College has not sent its best. And not nearly enough funds to cover further exploration. But we are making progress. My Charlemagne will be woken soon enough. And you shall have the credit of helping me find him.”=“你看看我们的工作成效！我有自己的学生了！有奖学金！有研究拨款！虽然——悄悄说——学院派来的可不是最顶尖的，而且拨款远远不够我们继续探索。但我们已经在进展中。我的查理曼很快就会醒来。到时你也能分享发现的荣耀。”
